- **oracle-author**: Authors the discriminating red-green test (the "oracle") for a high-blast-radius change FROM THE SPEC, blind to the implementation, BEFORE any code is written. Advisory role (not a commit gate); invoked by the plan-review `independent-oracle-high-blast-radius` rule. &lt;example&gt;Context: Plan involves a DB migration (high blast radius). user: "Author the oracle for this migration before I implement." assistant: "Running oracle-author to write the failing test from the spec, blind… ([sliamh11/Deus](https://github.com/sliamh11/Deus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oracle-author.md) · ★51)
- **oracle-author**: Writes the test that will judge a slice before the slice is implemented, from its proof obligation — claim, plausible failure, observable, oracle. Use when a planned slice has an obligation with no check that can fail, or when a human decision authorized rewriting a test that exists at HEAD. Writes only test files; never the implementation. ([usedevanity/skills](https://github.com/usedevanity/skills/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/oracle-author.md) · ★2)
- **oracle-author**: Turns one planned piece of work into a packet brief plus failing, machine-checkable oracle tests (parity test against a legacy capture, golden vector, invariant test or unit test) on branch packet/&lt;ID&gt;. Use before any implementer touches a packet, except security packets. Never implements the feature. ([Nevanyte/RogueHalla](https://github.com/Nevanyte/RogueHalla/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oracle-author.md))
- **oracle-awaiter**: Own one GPT-5.6-Sol-plus-Pro-first Oracle run, wait for terminal or harvested-complete status, and avoid impatience-driven reruns or model downgrades. ([ma08/botfiles](https://github.com/ma08/botfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/oracle-awaiter.md) · ★30)
- **oracle-awr-analyst**: Analyse an AWR report into evidence-backed findings ranked by % of DB Time and correlated to repository code. Use when given an AWR HTML/text file, a snapshot range, or asked why the database was slow over a period. Returns a structured findings report; refuses unrepresentative windows. ([ancyonio/OracleDBIQ](https://github.com/ancyonio/OracleDBIQ/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oracle-awr-analyst.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **oracle-behavioral-prover**: Oracle council member: behavioral prover — runs tests and produces reproducible evidence that implementation matches stated behavior. ([LucasQuiles/sdlc-os](https://github.com/LucasQuiles/sdlc-os/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-behavioral-prover.md) · ★1)
- **oracle-beta**: Strategic reviewer and adjudicator (best-of-N variant beta). Identical methodology to @oracle with model claude-opus-4-7 for vendor diversity. ([BB-84C/omo-slim-superpowers-patch-kit](https://github.com/BB-84C/omo-slim-superpowers-patch-kit/blob/HEAD/opencode-config/agents/oracle-beta.md) · ★14)
- **oracle-builder**: Turns approved spec scenarios and mined HAPI JUnit behaviours into executable differential and oracle test cases, written BEFORE any implementation exists. Use after spec-reviewer has written APPROVED and before implementer starts. ([gointerop/frevo](https://github.com/gointerop/frevo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/oracle-builder.md))
- **oracle-builder**: Leaf agent. The single owner of every full build in the pipeline — the pre-flight baseline build of the untouched tree, and the authoritative full-workspace build + test suite + coverage after each code wave. Builds multi-package workspaces in dependency order, captures the evidence scoped checks cannot, and exits with precise failure evidence — it never modifies source, never builds concurrently with writers. ([KarthikMAM/oracle](https://github.com/KarthikMAM/oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/oracle-builder.md))
- **oracle-bulk-processing-advisor**: Decide whether row-by-row PL/SQL should become set-based SQL or BULK COLLECT/FORALL, size LIMIT against PGA, and design SAVE EXCEPTIONS partial-failure handling. Use for cursor loops, batch migrations and "process millions of rows" work — and to refuse bulk processing where it would only add memory pressure. ([ancyonio/OracleDBIQ](https://github.com/ancyonio/OracleDBIQ/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/oracle-bulk-processing-advisor.agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **oracle-check**: Runs the Postman oracle (a local collection) against one candidate request URL via the Postman CLI, and reports pass/fail. It never writes code and must not read the collection. ([Postman-Devrel/booth-demos](https://github.com/Postman-Devrel/booth-demos/blob/HEAD/content/lightning-talks/open-meteo-loop-eng/app/.claude/agents/oracle-check.md) · ★2)
- **oracle-check-agent**: You are spawned by the `diagnose` skill in **Phase 0, before anything else**. Your job is to pin the exact symptom and then try to **falsify that it is a defect at all** — because a sound debugging loop run on a wrong expectation is more dangerous than ad-hoc guessing. You do not debug. You do not propose causes. You return a JSON verdict. - **orch-docs-specialist**: Edit docs, PRDs, deploy templates, archive moves, doc-index maintenance. Use for documentation changes. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/archive/inert-plugins-2026-05/05-docs-surface/agents/orch-docs-specialist.md) · ★1)

- **orch-doctor-specialist**: Edit doctor checks, CHECKS array, and pre-package load-bearing list. Use when adding/changing doctor checks. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/archive/inert-plugins-2026-05/03-observability/agents/orch-doctor-specialist.md) · ★1)

- **orch-implementer**: The ticket subagent of an orchestrator flow - builds exactly one ticket test-first on its ticket branch, commits, checks its own commits against the ticket's acceptance criteria and that a test exercises every source file they changed, and returns a six-line report. - **orch-intent-reviewer**: Intent reviewer for /orchestrate: does the change match the original intent and plan from the user's point of view. Use only from the orchestrate skill. ([Oywayten/claude-kit](https://github.com/Oywayten/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/payload/agents/orch-intent-reviewer.md))

- **orch-lens-implementability**: The Implementability lens of an orchestrator spec review - reads a spec issue's body and comments for its implementability from the issue alone, and writes its findings file and a code map of the locations the spec touches, carried to the next round, and no other path. - **orch-lens-testability**: The Testability lens of an orchestrator spec review - reads a spec issue's body and comments for its testability at the agreed seams, and writes its findings to one file. One source per call; the chair synthesizes across briefs. ([Sheldon-desouza/claude-code-orchestrator](https://github.com/Sheldon-desouza/claude-code-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-researcher.md))

- **orch-researcher-implementation-prd-author**: You are the researcher-implementation-prd-author. Sonnet-tier. - **orch-resolver**: Resolver/fixer for the orchestrate workflow — finishes merge-conflict resolutions, fixes stale tests or CI failures on a lane branch, keeping both sides' intent; runs only the touched tests; pushes. - **orch-resolver**: The resolver of an orchestrator merge conflict - a fresh subagent that finishes one in-progress merge or rebase in the checkout it is given, or for a ticket starts the rebase onto a named parent branch itself, by reading each side's primary sources, resolving each hunk by intent, never aborting, running the repo's checks, committing, and returning a four-line report. - **orch-reviewer**: Spawned by the Architect fresh after a dispatched issue's PR stops changing — reviews the PR against its issue's acceptance criteria and produces one consolidated verdict for orch run review. ([kninetimmy/orch](https://github.com/kninetimmy/orch/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/orch-reviewer.md) · ★22)
- **orch-reviewer**: Dispatched fresh after a Delivery PR stops changing to review it against the approved issue and return one consolidated verdict. ([kninetimmy/orch](https://github.com/kninetimmy/orch/blob/HEAD/adapters/opencode/agents/orch-reviewer.md) · ★22)
- **orch-reviewer**: Review a committed wave against its plan for problems. Reads and runs; never edits. ([Ramtoi/skill-tree](https://github.com/Ramtoi/skill-tree/blob/HEAD/tests/fixtures/ships_with/orchestrate-advanced/agents/orch-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **orch-reviewer**: Read-only diff review for scope-respect, drift, and paired-test coverage. Use post-integration before finalize. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/00-orchestrator/agents/orch-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **orch-reviewer**: Reach for this before shipping something expensive to get wrong — money moving, an auth boundary, destructive data, a contract other people consume. Send it when the change is pushed, keep the pull request a draft until the verdict is PASS. Judges the change against the questions you wrote before the build and returns PASS or FAIL with findings. Run it on Opus or stronger, never on the author's own weaker model. Not for a quick sanity check — do that yourself. ([1xmint/orchestrate](https://github.com/1xmint/orchestrate/blob/HEAD/skills/orchestrate/assets/agents/orch-reviewer.md))
- **orch-reviewer**: Reviewer for the orchestrate workflow — reviews one branch (or a delta) against its brief, runs only the changed tests, writes a verdict file whose first line is PASS or FAIL; fails only on HIGH issues or regressions; never modifies the branch. Use for review and delta-review steps dispatched by an orchestrator. ([baochunli/orchestrate](https://github.com/baochunli/orchestrate/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-reviewer.md))
- **orch-reviewer**: Read-only cross-model code/plan reviewer for the orchestrator skill. Use ONLY when the orchestrator dispatches a review brief (model and effort passed per call). - **orch-reviewer-spec**: The Spec axis of one orchestrator review - an iteration of a review loop, or a review pass - checks whether the whole change since a base SHA implements what the spec issue asked for, writes its findings unranked to a report file, and returns one line. Started only by the orch-review skill's driver, or by a review pass, with a base SHA, a spec issue, an iteration, a report path, the path of orch.sh, a known-items file, and the standards documents' list. - **orch-reviewer-standards**: The Standards axis of one orchestrator review - an iteration of a review loop, or a review pass - checks the whole change since a base SHA against the repo's documented coding standards, a fixed smell baseline and a doc-agreement check, writes its findings unranked to a report file, and returns one line. Started only by the orch-review skill's driver, or by a review pass, with a base SHA, a spec issue, an iteration, a report path, the path of orch.sh, a known-items file, and the standards documents' list. ([maj0rpain/orchestrator](https://github.com/maj0rpain/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/orch-reviewer-standards.md))

- **orch-scout**: Spawned by the Architect for read-only investigation before a plan is built, or when an escalation reroutes an issue to the scout role — locating code, gathering evidence, and answering "where/what/how" questions without changing anything. - **orch-tester-integration-author**: You are the tester-integration-author. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/docs/audits/2026-05-06-orchestrator-research/V3_PLUGIN_SETS/SET_B/02-tester/agents/orch-tester-integration-author.md) · ★1)
- **orch-tester-stub-rewriter**: You are the tester-stub-rewriter. ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/docs/audits/2026-05-06-orchestrator-research/V3_PLUGIN_SETS/SET_B/02-tester/agents/orch-tester-stub-rewriter.md) · ★1)
- **orch-tester-tdd-author**: You are the tester-tdd-author. The orchestrator dispatches you in TDD lockstep with `orch-implementer-coder` (or before, when implementer halted with no-peer-test). ([Jpk0512/ryu-next](https://github.com/Jpk0512/ryu-next/blob/HEAD/docs/audits/2026-05-06-orchestrator-research/V3_PLUGIN_SETS/SET_B/02-tester/agents/orch-tester-tdd-author.md) · ★1)

- **orchestration-analyst**: Multi-agent orchestration analyst. Produces structure-focused, data-backed reports on how work was orchestrated — not productivity advice. - **orchestration-patterns**: **案例背景。** 本章沿用合成的 build 42 诊断案例。用户问构建为什么失败；模型请求调用只读 tool（工具）`read_build_log`，应用核验权限后执行。工具调用 ID `c1` 把这次请求和返回结果关联起来：`unit-tests` 步骤中的 `reject_empty_name` 测试预期 HTTP（Hypertext Transfer Protocol，超文本传输协议）`400`，实际得到 `200`，进程退出状态为 `1`。回答这个问题只需要一个运行循环：读取证据，再判断何时可以作答。若把每个职责都拆成单独的智能体，既没有可并行的独立工作，又多出交接和校验成本。 ([Negronii/MyNotes](https://github.com/Negronii/MyNotes/blob/HEAD/ai-engineering/agents/orchestration-patterns.md) · ★2)

- **orchestration-planner**: Agent team composition planner. Spawn when you have a complex goal or task and need to determine the optimal combination of agents, their sequencing, handoff points, and parallelization strategy before executing. Use when the right agent team is not obvious, when multiple phases are involved, when a high-level requirement needs decomposing into a concrete execution plan, or when you want to avoid costly mid-task reclassification. Returns a structured orchestration plan the conductor can execute directly. Does not implement anything - planning only. - **orchestration-router**: Implementation dispatcher. Run in Plan Mode AFTER the plan is written to a file and BEFORE ExitPlanMode — IN A SEPARATE MESSAGE, once the plan reviewer batch (vibe-flow:plan-reviewer, vibe-flow:security-reviewer, vibe-flow:performance-reviewer and project ones) has returned and their sections are in the file. Reads the plan, classifies the work by a matrix (uniform mass / heterogeneous streams / trivial edit) and appends to the plan a "🚀 Implementation orchestration" section with a verdict: ▶️ direct execution, 🔀 agent crew (parallel groups of Agent calls; the TeamCreate tool if available) or… ([mosu1985/vibe-flow](https://github.com/mosu1985/vibe-flow/blob/HEAD/plugins/vibe-flow/agents/orchestration-router.md) · ★5)

- **orchestration-security-reviewer**: Independent security gate for a PR. A separate agent from the code reviewer, hunting only for data leaks, privilege escalation, and isolation/authorization breaks. Returns concise structured findings.
